- **seurat-de-testing**: Differential expression testing in R/Seurat (v5) — the practical method-choice recipe. Three paths — Path A FindAllMarkers (cluster markers, Wilcoxon), Path B FindMarkers (two-group test, optional MAST/LR with covariates), Path C AggregateExpression + DESeq2 (PSEUDOBULK for multi-sample condition effects). Includes the explicit ban on running bulk DE tools (DESeq2, edgeR, limma, pydeseq2) directly on per-cell scRNA matrices — those are bulk + pseudobulk tools only. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-findmarkers-workflow))
- **seurat-hto-assay-processing**: Use when a pooled multi-sample scRNA-seq experiment has a cell-hashing (HTO) assay stored in a Seurat object and cells must be assigned singlet/doublet/negative hashtag identities, especially with many hashtags or imbalanced sample sizes where cutoff calling dominates negative assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-hto-assay-processing))
- **seurat-htodemux-singlet-doublet-negative-calling**: Use when a cell-hashing CITE-seq experiment yields a hashtag oligo count matrix (e.g. from CITE-seq-Count) and cells must be assigned to hashed samples with doublet/negative calls, as in gExcite's hashing deconvolution step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-htodemux-singlet-doublet-negative-calling))
- **seurat-hvg-report**: Extract, tabulate and visualize the highly variable genes selected in Action 5 (VST), per condition, with cross-condition membership comparison; consumes the .rds outputs of seurat-normalize-hvg ([DNALinux/tonina](https://github.com/DNALinux/tonina/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes/seurat_hvg_report) · ★2)
- **seurat-integrate-rpca**: Select shared integration features, run per-sample PCA, and perform RPCA anchor integration (scRNA-seq workflow Actions 6-7); consumes the .rds outputs of seurat-normalize-hvg; integration itself is a user-confirmed decision ([DNALinux/tonina](https://github.com/DNALinux/tonina/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes/seurat_integrate_rpca) · ★2)
- **seurat-integration**: Multi-sample scRNA-seq integration with R/Seurat v5 — load N samples, merge, split the RNA assay into per-sample layers, preprocess (Normalize→HVG→Scale→PCA) on the layered object, then IntegrateLayers (CCAIntegration / RPCAIntegration / HarmonyIntegration / JointPCAIntegration / scVIIntegration / FastMNNIntegration) into a corrected reduction. Cluster + UMAP on the integrated reduction; JoinLayers before DE. The Seurat v5 layer-based replacement for v4's FindIntegrationAnchors / IntegrateData anchor flow. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-integration))
- **seurat-load-qc**: Load 10x count matrices into Seurat, apply cell-level QC, and save per-condition QC'd .rds objects (scRNA-seq workflow Actions 1-3); manifest-driven, any files and any conditions; study parameters are confirmed with the user before running ([DNALinux/tonina](https://github.com/DNALinux/tonina/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes/seurat_load_qc) · ★2)
- **seurat-local**: Seurat 文档本地镜像（Satija Lab docs，包含 seurat/ 子目录与 archive） ([Ketomihine/my_skills](https://github.com/Ketomihine/my_skills/tree/HEAD/seurat-local) · ★3)
- **seurat-local**: Seurat documentation (local mirror) ([Ketomihine/my_skills](https://github.com/Ketomihine/my_skills/tree/HEAD/seurat_official) · ★3)
- **seurat-log-count-preprocessing**: Use when raw UMI count matrices from two scRNA-seq batches (e.g., Muraro CEL-Seq2 and Segerstolpe Smart-Seq2 pancreas data) must be normalized to log-counts before batch correction or before computing Euclidean distances between cells in gene space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-log-count-preprocessing))
- **seurat-lognormalize-preprocessing**: Use when preparing UMI-based scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., HCA MantonCB1/MantonBM1 or CITE-seq PBMC) as input to cTP-net protein imputation, after SAVER-X denoising and before running the pre-trained 24-protein model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-lognormalize-preprocessing))
- **seurat-louvain-graph-clustering**: Use when benchmarking clustering methods on UMI-based simulated scRNA-seq data — especially detecting a rare subpopulation — and you need a graph-based baseline to compare against k-means on PCA/scVI latent spaces and SIMLR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-louvain-graph-clustering))
- **seurat-louvain-leiden-clustering**: Use when benchmarking a new scRNA-seq clustering framework (e.g., DeepCCI) against baselines, and you need Seurat Louvain/Leiden results on the same labeled datasets to compute comparative ARI/NMI scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-louvain-leiden-clustering))
- **seurat-marker-detection**: Use when comparing transcriptional profiles of barcode groups defined by different cell-calling methods (e.g., All-retained vs EmptyNN-retained vs EmptyNN-removed) from a raw scRNA-seq count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-marker-detection))
- **seurat-marker-detection-findallmarkers**: Use when you have a gene+TE (or feature-by-cell) expression matrix with cluster identities and need per-cluster marker TEs/genes with log2FC and adjusted P-values, or when benchmarking a quantification method's marker-recovery rate against known cluster labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-marker-detection-findallmarkers))
- **seurat-marker-gene-selection**: Use when you have a preprocessed count matrix plus manually annotated cell-type labels and need top positive marker genes per cell type to run marker-supervised methods (SCSA, MarkerCount, SCINA) or evaluate marker-based identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-marker-gene-selection))
- **seurat-multimodal-analysis**: Orchestrator for R/Seurat multimodal single-cell analysis — chains CITE-seq WNN, Signac chromatin analysis, bridge integration, and unpaired RNA+ATAC anchoring across four leaf skills. Use as the entry point when the project is R-based and must stay in Seurat. For the Python scverse use muon-multimodal-analysis. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/seurat-multimodal-analysis) · ★1)
- **seurat-multimodal-reference-mapping**: Use when a STARsolo-derived DGE has spatial barcodes too sparse (few UMIs each, e.g. SeqScope ~1M barcodes/mm2) for direct clustering, and cell-type labels must be transferred from a reference onto grid-collapsed spatial segments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-multimodal-reference-mapping))
- **seurat-multimodal-reference-mapping**: Map a scRNA-seq (or CITE-seq) query onto a pre-built multimodal reference in R/Seurat v5 — find SCT-flavoured anchors against the reference's supervised PCA (spca) with FindTransferAnchors(), then MapQuery() to transfer cell-type labels at MULTIPLE granularities (l1/l2/l3) AND impute predicted protein levels, all projected onto the reference's pre-computed WNN UMAP. Produces a query Seurat object with `predicted.celltype.l*` columns, per-cell `.score` confidences, a `predicted_ADT` assay, and a `ref.umap` reduction. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-multimodal-reference-mapping))
- **seurat-normalization-and-clustering**: Use when the input is a raw single-cell count matrix (e.g. from GEO accessions like GSE124395 or GSE63473, or 10x filtered_gene_bc_matrices) and you need normalized, clustered, UMAP-embedded cells as input to marker-based cell-type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-normalization-and-clustering))
- **seurat-normalization-dimensionality-reduction-clustering**: Use when raw spliced/unspliced gene-by-cell UMI matrices from the scKB/kallisto pipeline need non-arbitrary QC followed by Seurat normalization, PCA, UMAP, and clustering, e.g. Arabidopsis root datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-normalization-dimensionality-reduction-clustering))
- **seurat-normalize-hvg**: Normalize each QC'd sample (LogNormalize) and select highly variable genes per sample (VST), scRNA-seq workflow Actions 4-5; consumes the .rds outputs of seurat-load-qc ([DNALinux/tonina](https://github.com/DNALinux/tonina/tree/HEAD/skills/hermes/seurat_normalize_hvg) · ★2)
- **seurat-object-cluster-processing**: Use when you have a Seurat object with graph-based clustering already performed and need to extract a cluster-specific differential expression gene list for enrichment analysis with E.PAGE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-cluster-processing))
- **seurat-object-construction-and-manipulation**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI counts (e.g. 10x cellranger filtered_bc_matrix or gene-by-cell tables) must be converted into a Seurat object for QC, normalization, scaling and variable-feature detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-construction-and-manipulation))
- **seurat-object-construction-from-count-matrices**: Use when you have sci-L3-RNA and sci-L3-ATAC count matrices plus per-cell depth metrics (e.g. single_cell_summary_all.txt) and need cell objects for PCA/LSI, UMAP, and cell-type assignment against SNV ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/seurat-object-construction-from-count-matrices))
- **seurat-object-construction-from-spatial-dge**: Use when a DGE plus spatial coordinates at ultra-high resolution (e.g. Seq-Scope, &gt;1M barcodes/mm2 with very low UMI per barcode) must be aggregated into grid-level Seurat objects before dimension reduction and clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-construction-from-spatial-dge))
- **seurat-object-downstream-analysis**: Use when a preprocessed Seurat object from a plant single-cell experiment (e.g., Arabidopsis root scRNA-seq) is available and trajectory inference, cell-type annotation, deconvolution, or cross-species integration is the next analysis step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-downstream-analysis))
- **seurat-object-manipulation**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset is stored as a Seurat object and you need to subset cells, downsample, or attach per-cell signature scores (e.g. Tcell, Myeloid, NK, Plasma_cell) via AddModuleScore_UCell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-manipulation--6073b6151b8990d8))
- **seurat-object-manipulation**: Use when a Seurat-format scRNA-seq object must be normalized (LogNormalize, scale.factor=10000) and passed to scPagwas for pathway activation transformation, or when comparing gene-level vs pathway-level matrix sparsity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-manipulation--1549c698ed2fee44))
- **seurat-object-processing**: Use when a scRNA-seq dataset is stored as a Seurat object but the downstream doublet-detection tool (Chord/scds) requires a SingleCellExperiment object, or when preparing per-sample Seurat objects for Chord. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-processing))
- **seurat-object-single-cell-data-handling**: Use when working with plant scRNA-seq data (e.g., Arabidopsis thaliana root atlas from Zhang et al., 2019) inside the scPlant pipeline and you need to hold expression matrices, cluster labels, GSEA results, and topic models in one R object. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-single-cell-data-handling))
- **seurat-object-wrangling**: Use when multiple spatially resolved transcriptomics samples are held as Seurat objects (e.g. DLPFC Visium sections in seuList) and must be converted into an iSCMEBObj with an adjacency matrix list for iSC.MEB clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-object-wrangling))
- **seurat-pca-clustering-pipeline**: Use when you have a log-normalized single-cell Seurat object with a biologically informed feature set (e.g., DUBStepR optimal features) and need PCA-based graph clustering to resolve discrete cell subtypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-pca-clustering-pipeline))
- **seurat-pca-clustering-workflow**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq count matrices from PBMC (e.g. GEO accessions like GSE149689) must be clustered and cell-type annotated to determine which immune subsets show differential expression of genes such as MCEMP1 and HLA-DRA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-pca-clustering-workflow))
- **seurat-pseudobulk**: Performs pseudobulk differential expression analysis using Seurat v5 and the custom `anayet` helper function. Trigger when the user asks to "run pseudobulk DE," "aggregate counts," "compare treatment groups," or any variant of "DESeq2 on a Seurat object." Works with both single-cell RNA-seq and spatial transcriptomics (Xenium, Visium) data. ([arbones/claude-skills](https://github.com/arbones/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/seurat-pseudobulk))
- **seurat-reference-mapping**: Map a scRNA-seq query onto a labeled Seurat reference (R/Seurat v5) — find transfer anchors between reference and query, transfer cell-type labels and metadata via TransferData, and project query cells into the reference's PCA + UMAP space via MapQuery. The reference's UMAP model must be saved with return.model = TRUE. Covers the generic case (user-supplied reference Seurat object) and points at Azimuth for pre-built tissue references. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-reference-mapping))
- **seurat-rna-atac-integration**: Joint scRNA + scATAC analysis on a 10x Multiome dataset with R/Seurat (v5) + Signac — build ONE Seurat object holding an RNA assay AND a Signac ChromatinAssay, preprocess each modality separately (RNA→SCTransform→PCA; ATAC→TF-IDF→SVD/LSI), fuse them with `FindMultiModalNeighbors` (WNN), cluster + UMAP on the weighted graph, and add a gene-activity matrix + CoveragePlot diagnostics. The RNA+ATAC specialization of the general WNN pattern. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-rna-atac-integration))
- **seurat-sample-integration-clustering**: Use when multi-patient scRNA-seq samples (e.g., 2 Pre-T ALL patients vs 3 healthy controls from GSE132509) must be integrated across batches, clustered, and cell-type-annotated before per-cluster differential expression and drug scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-sample-integration-clustering))
- **seurat-scrna-preprocessing**: Use when a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. Hydra dataset counts, Tabula Muris marrow counts, PBMC GSE132044 matrices) must be converted into a normalized, scaled, PCA-reduced Seurat object before running t-SNE/UMAP hyperparameter optimization or embedding-quality assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-scrna-preprocessing))
- **seurat-scrna-v2**: Single-sample scRNA-seq QC + clustering + markers with R/Seurat v5 — Read10X/Read10X_h5 → CreateSeuratObject → percent.mt + nFeature/nCount QC subset → NormalizeData/FindVariableFeatures/ScaleData → RunPCA + ElbowPlot → FindNeighbors/FindClusters (Louvain) → RunUMAP → FindAllMarkers (Wilcoxon). Default canonical Seurat path; SCTransform sibling recipe handles low-depth / regularized-NB normalization. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-scrna-v2))
- **seurat-scrnaseq-clustering**: Use when starting from raw 10x scRNA-seq counts (e.g., Cell Ranger filtered_gene_bc_matrices or an RDS Seurat object) and unsupervised clusters are needed as input for ScType marker-based cell-type annotation or SNV-score assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-scrnaseq-clustering))
- **seurat-scrnaseq-preprocessing**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count data (e.g. Tabula Muris thymus) must be normalized, HVG-selected, scaled, and embedded via PCA before trajectory or DEG analysis downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-scrnaseq-preprocessing))
- **seurat-sctransform**: Single-sample scRNA-seq workflow with SCTransform regularized-NB normalization (R/Seurat v5) — replaces NormalizeData + FindVariableFeatures + ScaleData with one SCTransform() call, downstream PCA/UMAP/clustering/markers run on the SCT assay, and PrepSCTFindMarkers gates DE on merged multi-model objects. Preferred when sequencing depth is low or varies a lot across cell types. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-sctransform))
- **seurat-single-cell-analysis**: R-based single cell analysis using Seurat v5 for .rds/.rdata files ([Ketomihine/my_skills](https://github.com/Ketomihine/my_skills/tree/HEAD/seurat-single-cell) · ★3)
- **seurat-single-cell-analysis**: Use when raw single-cell RNA-seq counts (e.g., 10x PBMC, bone marrow, MERFISH spatial) must be normalized, clustered, and embedded in Seurat before cell-type annotation with accordion() or accordion_disease(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-single-cell-analysis))
- **seurat-single-cell-analyzer**: Seurat single-cell analysis skill for clustering, annotation, and trajectory analysis ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/specializations/domains/science/bioinformatics/skills/seurat-single-cell-analyzer) · ★1.9k)
- **seurat-single-cell-clustering**: Use when you have two adjusted gene-barcode UMI matrices from paired single-cell processing pipelines and need to compare their cell-clustering outcomes quantitatively via ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-single-cell-clustering))
- **seurat-single-cell-object-handling**: Use when running Chord or its built-in detectors (scds, DoubletFinder) on scRNA-seq data: the input must be a Seurat object, and scds additionally requires an sce obtained via creatSCE(seu). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-single-cell-object-handling))
- **seurat-umap-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., GEO-deposited 10x-style BALF counts) must be reduced to a low-dimensional embedding for clustering and cell-type annotation before severe-vs-mild differential expression testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-umap-dimensionality-reduction))
- **seurat-unpaired-cross-modality**: Seurat unpaired RNA-ATAC integration via gene-activity anchoring — CCA-based FindTransferAnchors, TransferData label transfer, and exploratory expression imputation for ATAC cells. Use with separate scRNA and scATAC experiments (NOT true multiome) and no bridge dataset. For the Python equivalent use scglue-unpaired-multiomics-integration. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/seurat-unpaired-cross-modality) · ★1)
- **seurat-v3-standard-workflow**: Use when a sparse count matrix of single-nucleus droplets (e.g., 10X mouse brain snRNA-seq) has been pre-filtered by DIEM, EmptyDrops, or a quantile-based hard count cutoff and you need to check for residual background/debris clusters downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-v3-standard-workflow))
- **seurat-v5**: Work with a Seurat v5 project using Assay5 layers, sample integration, WNN multimodal graphs, or sketch/large-data workflows. Use when these v5 contracts are relevant, rather than for every R single-cell task. ([CHENyiru3/AI-Skills-Collections](https://github.com/CHENyiru3/AI-Skills-Collections/tree/HEAD/skills-market/compbio/single-cell/integration/seurat-v5) · ★3)
- **seurat-wilcoxon-marker-detection**: Use when computing case-vs-control differential expression per cell cluster from integrated Seurat objects as input to drug repurposing, or when benchmarking DE methods (Wilcoxon vs Limma/DESeq2/edgeR) by downstream AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-wilcoxon-marker-detection))
- **seurat-wnn-multimodal**: Weighted Nearest Neighbor (WNN) multimodal clustering in R/Seurat v5 — process each modality independently (RNA + ADT, or RNA + ATAC) to get its own dimensional reduction, then learn cell-specific modality weights with FindMultiModalNeighbors() and cluster on the joint wsnn graph. Produces one shared UMAP where the modalities' contributions are weighted per cell, plus a per-cell `RNA.weight` metadata column revealing which cells are RNA-driven vs protein/chromatin-driven. ([kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack](https://github.com/kharchenkolab/aba-recipe-pack/tree/HEAD/recipes/genomics/seurat-wnn-multimodal))
- **seurat-wnn-multimodal-clustering-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking a decision-level or ensemble single-cell integration method against a joint-latent-space baseline, especially for RNA plus a second modality (splicing PSI, ADT, ATAC) where modality-specific subclusters are expected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/seurat-wnn-multimodal-clustering-benchmarking))
- **seurat-workflow-orchestration-for-scrnaseq**: Use when you have a raw or Seurat object-backed scRNA-seq expression matrix and need to: (1) stabilize variance across genes with SCTransform normalization, (2) extract feature loadings in reduced dimensionality space via reverse PCA to use as input for GESECA or other coregulation-based enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/seurat-workflow-orchestration-for-scrnaseq))
- **seuratclustering**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/seuratclustering) · ★745)
- **seuratclustering**: Performs unsupervised clustering on single-cell RNA-seq data using Seurat. This process finds nearest neighbors, computes UMAP for visualization, and applies Louvain/Leiden algorithms to identify cell clusters. Clusters can be explored at multiple resolutions to balance granularity and biological relevance. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry-data/tree/HEAD/ai-ml/seuratclustering-pwwang-immunopipe-750e0e96) · ★30 · 3 copies)
- **seuratclustering-pwwang-immunopipe-750e0e96**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/seuratclustering-pwwang-immunopipe-750e0e96) · ★745)
- **seuratclusteringofallcells**: Performs coarse clustering on ALL cells (including T cells, B cells, and non-T/B cells) before cell type selection.
